Let's design a practical algorithm with multiple strategies:
1. Generate initial candidates:
   - Uniform length maybe 2535.
   - Binomial b_j = C(2j,j)/4^j normalized (can compute recursively to avoid overflow): b0=1, b_{j+1}=b_j*(2j+1)/(2j+2)? Actually C(2j+2,j+1)=C(2j,j)*(2j+2)(2j+1)/(j+1)^2? Divided by 4: b_{j+1}=b_j * (2j+1)/(2j+2). Let's verify: b_j=C(2j,j)/4^j. b_{j+1}=C(2j+2,j+1)/4^{j+1}=C(2j,j)*(2j+2)(2j+1)/(j+1)^2 /4^{j+1}=b_j * (2j+2)(2j+1)/(4(j+1)^2)=b_j*(2j+1)/(2j+2). Yes. b decreases after j=0 from 1 to 0.5 etc.
   - Binomial with small modulation: w_j = 1 + α cos(2π j/n) etc, ensure nonnegative.
   - Truncated power law: a_j=(j+1)^-β normalized for β sweep.
   - Beta density: a_j ∝ ((j+1)/(n-j))^(-β) maybe.
   - Random nonnegative normalized.

Line search robust:
```
def step_and_search(a, grad, eta0):
    best=a, bestF=F(a)
    for eta in loggrid(eta0, eta0*1e-6, 10 steps):
       v = a - eta*grad
       v = project_simplex(v)
       f = F_fast(v)
       if f < bestF: update
```

FFT objective:
```
def conv_max_fft(a):
    n = len(a)
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < 2*n-1: nfft <<=1
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f*f, n=nfft)[:2*n-1]
    # numerical neg small
    return np.max(c)
```
For n=2048 nfft=4096. Very fast. For variable n maybe power of two.

Need build base b: `b = np.empty(n); b[0]=1; for j in 1..n-1: b[j]=b[j-1]*(2*j-1)/(2*j)` (since j index, from b_{j-1} to b_j multiply (2(j-1)+1)/(2(j-1)+2) = (2j-1)/(2j)). Sum S. Normalize? We can keep unnormalized b and build a = b*w; normalize each candidate. Sum may be large ~56 for n=2048, safe. Values b0=1, b1=0.5, etc; with β up to1, values up to2. a unnormalized max2; conv max ~? fine.

Candidate creation:
```
def make_mod_candidate(b, v, beta):
    w = 1.0 + beta*v
    # clip negative to 0 to be safe
    if w.min() <0: w=np.clip(w,0,None)
    a = b*w
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=0: return uniform
    a = a/s
    return a
```
If w has small negative due β, clipping changes mean; okay.

    grid = np.linspace(0,1.0,201) (or 1.2)
    best_a,best_score

def score_from_a(a):
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return inf
    # use FFT conv
    c = fft_conv(a)
    return 2*len(a)*np.max(c)/(s*s)
```

Let's detail binomial unnormalized:
```
def binomial_base(n):
    b=np.empty(n,float); b[0]=1.0
    for j in range(1,n): b[j]=b[j-1]*(2*j-1)/(2*j)
    return b
```

Basis matrix:
```
def build_basis(n):
    x = np.arange(n)/(n-1)
    t = 2*x -1
    s1 = np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
    s2 = np.sin(4*np.pi*x)
    s3 = np.sin(6*np.pi*x)
    c1 = np.cos(np.pi*x)
    c2 = np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
    q = (x-0.5)**2
    basis = np.column_stack([t,s1,s2,s3,c1,c2,q])
    return basis
```

To construct candidate from coefficients c and beta:
```
def candidate_from_params(basis,b,S,c,beta):
    v0 = basis @ c
    mean = np.dot(b, v0)/S
    v = v0 - mean
    std = np.sqrt(np.dot(b, v*v)/S)
    if std < 1e-8: return None, 0.0
    v = v / std
    # beta max for nonnegative w=1+beta*v
    vmin = v.min()
    if vmin < 0:
       max_beta = -1.0/vmin
       if beta > max_beta: beta = max_beta
       if beta > 0.999*max_beta: beta = 0.999*max_beta
    # if beta negative? search uses positive; if beta<0, allow? Nonneg with positive v? max beta negative? We'll restrict beta>=0.
    if beta <= 0: return None,0
    w = 1.0 + beta*v
    # Due beta cap, w should >=0; clip tiny
    if w.min() < 0: w = np.clip(w,0,None)
    a = b*w
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return None,0
    a = a/s
    return a,beta
```
Because v unit RMS, beta cap maybe ~1/(-minv). If v min -2, maxβ0.5. Effect β_rms=β. To allow stronger, we can scale normalization differently: normalize v to max abs=1? Then beta can up to1 and RMS variable. But search over beta and shape can adapt. Unit RMS is fine; beta effect measured.

If beta cap small, candidate small effect; search may try larger beta but capped. We can also allow clipping and beta up to fixed 2 to create more endpoint zeros? Simpler: normalize v by max abs to ≤1, then beta ∈[0,1] ensures nonnegative. Effect = beta*std. Search can use beta. Let's do max normalization to have clear nonneg constraint. v = v / max(1, np.max(np.abs(v))) such that max≤1. Then beta in [0,1]. The RMS varies; search finds beta. This may be more intuitive. ```
def candidate_from_raw_v(raw_v, base, S, beta, nfft):
    mean = np.dot(base, raw_v)/S
    v = raw_v - mean
    vmax = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
    if vmax < 1e-12: return None, inf, 0.0
    v = v / vmax
    # ensure max negative not below -1 (should)
    # If beta > 1, cap 1
    beta = float(np.clip(beta, 0.0, 1.0))
    w = 1.0 + beta * v
    if w.min() < 0:
       w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    a = base * w
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300: return None, inf, beta
    a = a/s
    sc = objective_internal(a,nfft)
    return a, sc, beta
```
For coeffs: raw_v = basis @ coeffs.
For deterministic v: raw_v = v.

def run_param_search(base,S,n,basis,nfft,best_a,best_score,best_raw,best_beta,deadline,rng):
    K=basis.shape[1]
    if best_raw is not None:
        c_best, residuals, rank, sv = np.linalg.lstsq(basis, best_raw, rcond=None)
        c_best = np.real(c_best)
    else: c_best = rng.normal(0,0.5,K)
    beta_best = best_beta if best_beta>0 else 0.5
    sigma=0.25
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       if rng.random()<0.03: ...
       else: c = c_best + rng.normal(0,sigma,K); beta=...; 
       raw = basis @ c
       cand,sc,beta_used = candidate_from_raw_v(raw, base,S,beta,nfft)
       if cand is not None and sc < best_score:
          update; c_best=c; beta_best=beta_used; best_raw=raw; # maybe sigma = 0.95*sigma? increase? We can keep.
       it+=1
       if it%5000==0: print
    return ...

Local multiplicative search:

def run_local_search(base,n,nfft,best_a,best_score,deadline,rng):
    a=best_a.copy(); sc=best_score
    sigma=0.05
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       if it%1000==0: sigma = 0.02 + 0.06*rng.random()
       if rng.random()<0.5:
           noise = rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
           cand = a*np.exp(noise)
       else:
           cand = a.copy()
           width = int(rng.integers(max(1,n//50), max(2,n//10)))
           start = int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
           cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma*2,width))
       cand = np.maximum(cand,0)
       s=cand.sum()
       if s>0: cand/=s; score=objective_internal(cand,nfft); if score<sc: update
       it+=1
    return ...
```

Let's outline final code in detail.

```
import numpy as np
import time

def binomial_base(n):
    out=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    out[0]=1.0
    for j in range(1,n):
        out[j]=out[j-1]*(2.0*j-1.0)/(2.0*j)
    return out

def base_sequence(base,nfft):
    s=base.sum()
    if s <=0: return np.ones_like(base)
    a=base/s
    return a

def conv_max_fft(a,nfft):
    f=np.fft.rfft(a,n=nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f,n=nfft)
    m=len(a)
    c=c[:2*m-1]
    # remove roundoff negative
    if c.size:
       mn=c.min()
       if mn < 0:
          c[c<0]=0.0 # This modifies copy? c new
    return float(np.max(c))

def objective_internal(a,nfft):
    m=a.size
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12: return float('inf')
    cm=conv_max_fft(a,nfft)
    return float(2.0*m*cm/(s*s))
```
The `c[c<0]=0` if all negative? Not. It creates c from irfft writable. Good.

`candidate_from_raw_v`:
```
def candidate_from_raw_v(raw_v, base, S, beta, nfft):
    n=base.size
    if raw_v is None:
       a=base_sequence(base,nfft); sc=objective_internal(a,nfft); return a,sc,float(beta)
    v=raw_v - float(np.dot(base, raw_v)/S)
    vmax=float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
    if vmax < 1e-12:
       a=base_sequence(base,nfft); sc=objective_internal(a,nfft); return a,sc,float(beta)
    v = v / vmax
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta < 0: beta = 0.0
    if beta > 1.0: beta = 1.0
    w = 1.0 + beta * v
    # If tiny neg, clip
    if w.min() < 0.0:
       w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    a = base * w
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
       return None, float('inf'), beta
    a = a / s
    sc = objective_internal(a,nfft)
    return a, sc, beta
```
If raw_v has min less than -vmax, after normalization min≥-1. β≤1 nonneg.

`zero_mean_mode`:
```
def zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s):
    mt=float(np.dot(base,t)/S)
    ms=float(np.dot(base,s)/S)
    if abs(ms) < 1e-8:
       # return t centered?
       return t - mt  # but candidate subtracts mean again, becomes zero? Actually raw t, candidate subtracts mean -> t-mt.
    return t - (mt/ms) * s
```
If ms small, raw t will be mean-centered in candidate, effective t-mt (bad but okay).

`consider_top`:
```
def consider_top(top, a, sc, max_top=5):
    if a is None or not np.isfinite(sc): return
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <=1e-12: return
    arr=arr/s
    if len(top) >= max_top and sc > top[-1][0]: return
    i=0
    inserted=False
    while i < len(top):
       if sc < top[i][0]:
          top.insert(i,(float(sc),arr.copy()))
          inserted=True
          break
       i+=1
    if not inserted:
       if len(top)<max_top:
          top.append((float(sc),arr.copy()))
       else:
          # should not happen due earlier, but replace worst
          top[-1]=(float(sc),arr.copy())
    if len(top)>max_top:
       top.pop()
```

`run_deterministic_grid`:
```
def run_deterministic_grid(base,S,n,nfft,best_a,best_score):
    top=[]? We'll pass top.
```
Maybe put top in propose and call `consider_top` inside grid. But run_deterministic can also update best.

```
def update_best(top, best_a, best_score, a, sc):
   if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
       best_a = a.copy(); best_score = sc
   consider_top(top,a,sc,5)
   return best_a,best_score
```
Top-level no lambdas.

In grid:
```
x = np.arange(n)/float(n-1)
t = 2.0*x-1.0
s1 = np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x)
s2 = np.sin(4.0*np.pi*x)
s3 = np.sin(6.0*np.pi*x)
s4 = np.sin(8.0*np.pi*x)
# base
a,sc,_=candidate_from_raw_v(None,base,S,0.0,nfft); update
modes = [s1,s2,s3,s4]
betas_fine = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,121)
for s in modes:
   v = zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s)
   for beta in betas_fine:
      a,sc,bu=candidate_from_raw_v(v,base,S,beta,nfft); update
# combos of s1 and s2 or s1,s3
v1=zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s1)
v2=zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s2)
v3=zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s3)
for c in (-1.0,-0.5,0.0,0.5,1.0):
   v = v1 + c*v2
   for beta in np.linspace(0.0,1.0,61): update
   v = v1 + c*v3
   for beta in np.linspace(0.0,1.0,41): update
return best_a,best_score

```
def run_param_search(basis,base,S,n,nfft,best_a,best_score,best_raw,best_beta,stop_time,rng):
    K=basis.shape[1]
    if best_raw is None:
       c = rng.normal(0.0,0.5,K)
    else:
       sol = np.linalg.lstsq(basis,best_raw,rcond=None)
       c = sol[0]
    if best_beta > 0.02: beta=best_beta
    else: beta=0.5
    sigma=0.25
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
       if rng.random() < 0.04:
          c_new = rng.normal(0.0,0.8,K)
          beta_new = float(rng.uniform(0.05,1.0))
       else:
          c_new = c + rng.normal(0.0,sigma,K)
          beta_new = beta + float(rng.normal(0.0,0.08))
          if beta_new < 0.0 or beta_new > 1.0:
              if rng.random()<0.5: beta_new = float(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0))
              else: beta_new = float(np.clip(beta_new,0.0,1.0))
       raw = basis @ c_new
       a,sc,beta_used = candidate_from_raw_v(raw,base,S,beta_new,nfft)
       if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best_a = a.copy(); best_score = sc; c = c_new.copy(); beta = beta_used; best_raw=raw.copy()
          # adapt maybe sigma *= 1.05? not necessary
       it += 1
       if it % 20000 == 0:
          print('param it', it, 'best', best_score, flush=True)
    return best_a,best_score,best_raw,beta
```

def run_local_search(base,n,nfft,best_a,best_score,stop_time,rng,top):
    a=best_a.copy(); sc=best_score
    sigma=0.04
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
       if it % 500 ==0:
           sigma = float(0.02 + 0.08*rng.random())
       kind = rng.random()
       if kind < 0.6:
           noise = rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n)
           cand = a * np.exp(noise)
       else:
           cand = a.copy()
           width = int(rng.integers(max(1,n//64), max(2,n//8)))
           start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
           stop = min(n,start+width)
           cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*1.5, stop-start))
           if rng.random()<0.3:
              cand = 0.5*(cand + cand[::-1])? Not, symmetry may hurt. skip.
       cand = np.maximum(cand,0.0)
       s=cand.sum()
       if s > 0:
           cand/=s
           score=objective_internal(cand,nfft)
           if score < sc:
               a=cand; sc=score; best_a=a.copy(); best_score=sc; consider_top(top,cand,sc,5)
       it+=1
       if it%20000==0: print
    return best_a,best_score

```
import numpy as np
import time

def binomial_base(n):
    b=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    b[0]=1.0
    for j in range(1,n):
        b[j] = b[j-1] * ((2.0*j - 1.0)/(2.0*j))
    return b
...
```

`objective_internal` with nfft:
```
def objective_internal(a,nfft):
    m = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-14: return inf
    f=np.fft.rfft(a,n=nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f,n=nfft)
    c = c[:2*m-1]
    # If any nan? return inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): return inf
    mval = float(np.max(c))
    if mval < 0: mval=0.0
    return float(2.0*m*mval/(s*s))
```
No separate conv_max function needed. But candidate_from_raw calls objective. Deterministic base etc. We can inline.

Potential repeated allocation of `c` and `f`; okay.

`candidate_from_raw_v`:
```
def candidate_from_raw_v(raw_v, base, S, beta, nfft):
    n=base.size
    if raw_v is None:
       a=base.copy(); s=S; a /= s; sc=objective_internal(a,nfft); return a,sc,float(beta)
    v = raw_v.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    mean = float(np.dot(base, v) / S)
    v -= mean
    vmax = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
    if not np.isfinite(vmax) or vmax < 1e-12:
       a=base.copy(); a /= S; sc=objective_internal(a,nfft); return a,sc,float(beta)
    v /= vmax
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta < 0.0: beta=0.0
    if beta > 1.0: beta=1.0
    w = 1.0 + beta * v
    # because v max <=1, beta<=1, w>=0 except floating
    wmin = float(np.min(w))
    if wmin < 0.0:
        w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    a = base * w
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None, float('inf'), beta
    a /= s
    sc = objective_internal(a,nfft)
    return a, sc, beta
```
If raw_v is a view, `.astype(copy=True)` ensures writable. If raw_v is None.

`zero_mean_mode`:
```
def zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s):
    mt = float(np.dot(base,t)/S)
    ms = float(np.dot(base,s)/S)
    if abs(ms) < 1e-10:
        return t.copy()
    return t - (mt/ms)*s
```

`consider_top` as above.

```
def update_best(top,best_a,best_score,a,sc,best_raw,best_beta, raw_v, beta_used):
    if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_score = sc
        if raw_v is None:
           best_raw = np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
           best_raw = np.asarray(raw_v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        best_beta = float(beta_used)
    consider_top(top,a,sc,5)
    return best_a,best_score,best_raw,best_beta
```

def run_deterministic_grid(base,S,n,nfft):
    top=[]
    best_a=None; best_score=np.inf; best_raw=None; best_beta=0.0
    base_a, base_sc, _ = candidate_from_raw_v(None, base,S,0.0,nfft)
    best_a,best_score,best_raw,best_beta = update_best(top,... base_a,base_sc, None,0.0)
    x = np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)/float(n-1)
    t = 2.0*x -1.0
    s1 = np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x)
    s2 = np.sin(4.0*np.pi*x)
    s3 = np.sin(6.0*np.pi*x)
    s4 = np.sin(8.0*np.pi*x)
    betas_fine = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,121)
    for s in (s1,s2,s3,s4):
       v = zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s)
       for beta in betas_fine:
           a,sc,be = candidate_from_raw_v(v,base,S,beta,nfft)
           best_a,best_score,best_raw,best_beta = update_best(top,best_a,best_score,a,sc,v,be)
    v1 = zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s1)
    v2 = zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s2)
    v3 = zero_mean_mode(base,S,t,s3)
    betas_med = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,61)
    for cc in (-1.0,-0.5,0.0,0.5,1.0):
       v = v1 + cc*v2
       for beta in betas_med: ...
       v = v1 + cc*v3
       betas_small = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,41)
       for beta in betas_small: ...
    print('deterministic best', best_score, flush=True)
    return top,best_a,best_score,best_raw,best_beta
```

`run_param_search`:

```
def run_param_search(basis, base, S, nfft, best_a, best_score, best_raw, best_beta, stop_time, rng, top):
    K = basis.shape[1]
    c = None
    if best_raw is not None:
       try:
          sol = np.linalg.lstsq(basis, best_raw, rcond=None)
          c = np.asarray(sol[0], dtype=np.float64).copy()
       except Exception:
          c = None
    if c is None:
       c = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, K)
    beta = float(best_beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta < 0.02:
       beta = 0.5
    sigma = 0.25
    it=0
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
       r = float(rng.random())
       if r < 0.05:
          c_new = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, K)
          beta_new = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 1.0))
       else:
          c_new = c + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, K)
          beta_new = beta + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08))
          if beta_new <= 0.0 or beta_new >= 1.0:
             if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
                beta_new = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
             else:
                beta_new = float(np.clip(beta_new, 0.0, 1.0))
       raw = basis @ c_new
       a, sc, beta_used = candidate_from_raw_v(raw, base, S, beta_new, nfft)
       if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best_a = a.copy(); best_score = sc; c = c_new.copy(); beta = beta_used; best_raw = raw.copy()
          consider_top(top, a, sc, 5)
          no_improve = 0
          # maybe slightly increase sigma to exploit
          sigma = min(0.6, sigma*1.02)
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > 5000:
             sigma = min(0.8, sigma*1.1)
             no_improve = 0
       it += 1
       if it % 20000 == 0:
          print('param', it, best_score, flush=True)
    return best_a,best_score,best_raw,beta
```

```
def run_local_search(base,n,nfft,best_a,best_score,stop_time,rng,top):
    a=best_a.copy(); sc=best_score
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
       if it % 1000 == 0:
          sigma = float(0.02 + 0.08*float(rng.random()))
       if float(rng.random()) < 0.65:
          noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
          cand = a * np.exp(noise)
       else:
          cand = a.copy()
          width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n//64), max(2, n//8)))
          start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
          stop = min(n, start+width)
          if stop > start:
              cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*1.5, stop-start))
       cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
       s = float(np.sum(cand))
       if s > 1e-14:
          cand /= s
          score = objective_internal(cand, nfft)
          if score < sc:
             a = cand.copy(); sc = score; best_a = cand.copy(); best_score = score
             consider_top(top, cand, score, 5)
       it +=1
       if it % 20000 ==0: print
    return best_a,best_score
```

`exact_evaluate_top`:
```
def exact_evaluate_top(top, default_a):
    best_a = None; best_sc = float('inf')
    for item in top:
       sc, a = item
       try:
          exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
       except Exception:
          exact = float('inf')
       if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_sc:
          best_sc = float(exact); best_a = a.copy()
    if best_a is None:
       try:
          exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in default_a])
       except Exception:
          exact = float('inf')
       if np.isfinite(exact):
          best_sc=exact; best_a=default_a.copy()
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=default_a.copy()
    return best_a
```
Need ensure `a` not None. Top items contain a.

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1800,**kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1800.0
    if budget < 30: budget=30.0
    # maybe cap to avoid too long
    deadline = start + max(30.0, budget - 20.0)
    n = 4096
    nfft=1
    target_len = 2*n -1
    while nfft < target_len: nfft <<=1
    base = binomial_base(n)
    S = float(np.sum(base))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <=0:
       arr = [1.0/256.0]*256
       return arr
    top,best_a,best_score,best_raw,best_beta = run_deterministic_grid(base,S,n,nfft)
    print('after deterministic', best_score, flush=True)
    basis = build_basis(n)
    param_stop = deadline - 90.0
    if param_stop < start + 10: param_stop = deadline - 10.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if time.monotonic() < param_stop:
       best_a,best_score,best_raw,best_beta = run_param_search(basis,base,S,nfft,best_a,best_score,best_raw,best_beta,param_stop,rng,top)
    # maybe update local with remaining
    local_stop = deadline
    if time.monotonic() < local_stop and best_a is not None:
       local_deadline = local_stop
       # reserve 5 sec
       local_deadline = local_stop - 5.0
       if time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
          best_a,best_score = run_local_search(base,n,nfft,best_a,best_score,local_deadline,rng,top)
    # final exact
    best_a = exact_evaluate_top(top, best_a)
    # ensure nonnegative list
    arr = np.asarray(best_a,dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 1e-12:
       arr = np.ones(256)/256
    else:
       arr = arr / s
    # cap 1000 not needed
    return [float(x) for x in arr]
```

Helper `build_basis`:
```
def build_basis(n):
    x = np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)/float(n-1)
    t = 2.0*x - 1.0
    b = np.column_stack((t, t*t*t,
                          np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x),
                          np.sin(4.0*np.pi*x),
                          np.sin(6.0*np.pi*x),
                          np.cos(np.pi*x),
                          np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x),
                          (x-0.5)*(x-0.5)))
    return b.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def binomial_base(n):
    b = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 1.0
    for j in range(1, n):
        b[j] = b[j - 1] * ((2.0 * j - 1.0) / (2.0 * j))
    return b


def build_basis(n):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    b1 = t
    b2 = t * t * t
    b3 = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    b4 = np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    b5 = np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x)
    b6 = np.cos(np.pi * x)
    b7 = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    b8 = (x - 0.5) * (x - 0.5)
    return np.column_stack((b1, b2, b3, b4, b5, b6, b7, b8)).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def objective_internal(a, nfft):
    m = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-14:
        return float('inf')

    f = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, n=nfft)
    c = c[:2 * m - 1]

    if c.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return float('inf')

    peak = float(np.max(c))
    if peak < 0.0:
        peak = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * m * peak / (s * s))


def normalized_direction(raw_v, base, S):
    if raw_v is None:
        return np.zeros(base.size, dtype=np.float64)

    v = np.asarray(raw_v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    mean = float(np.dot(base, v) / S)
    v -= mean

    vmax = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
    if not np.isfinite(vmax) or vmax < 1e-12:
        return np.zeros(base.size, dtype=np.float64)

    v /= vmax
    return v


def candidate_from_normalized_v(v, base, beta, nfft):
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta < 0.0:
        beta = 0.0
    if beta > 1.0:
        beta = 1.0

    w = 1.0 + beta * v
    if w.size and float(np.min(w)) < 0.0:
        w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)

    a = base * w
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None, float('inf'), beta

    a /= s
    sc = objective_internal(a, nfft)
    return a, sc, beta


def candidate_from_raw_v(raw_v, base, S, beta, nfft):
    if raw_v is None:
        v = normalized_direction(None, base, S)
        a, sc, beta_used = candidate_from_normalized_v(v, base, 0.0, nfft)
        return a, sc, beta_used, v

    v = normalized_direction(raw_v, base, S)
    a, sc, beta_used = candidate_from_normalized_v(v, base, beta, nfft)
    return a, sc, beta_used, v


def consider_top(top, a, sc, max_top=5):
    if a is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
        return

    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return

    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return
    arr /= s

    if len(top) >= max_top and sc > top[-1][0]:
        return

    inserted = False
    i = 0
    while i < len(top):
        if sc < top[i][0]:
            top.insert(i, (float(sc), arr.copy()))
            inserted = True
            break
        i += 1

    if not inserted:
        if len(top) < max_top:
            top.append((float(sc), arr.copy()))
        else:
            top[-1] = (float(sc), arr.copy())

    if len(top) > max_top:
        top.pop()


def update_best(top, best_a, best_score, a, sc, v_dir, beta_used):
    if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        best_score = float(sc)
        if v_dir is None:
            best_dir = np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            best_dir = np.asarray(v_dir, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    else:
        best_dir = None

    consider_top(top, a, sc, 5)
    return best_a, best_score, best_dir


def zero_mean_mode(base, S, t, s):
    mt = float(np.dot(base, t) / S)
    ms = float(np.dot(base, s) / S)
    if abs(ms) < 1e-10:
        return t.copy()
    return t - (mt / ms) * s


def run_deterministic_grid(base, S, n, nfft):
    top = []
    best_a = None
    best_score = float('inf')
    best_dir = None

    a, sc, beta_used, v = candidate_from_raw_v(None, base, S, 0.0, nfft)
    best_a, best_score, best_dir = update_best(top, best_a, best_score, a, sc, v, beta_used)

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    s1 = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    s2 = np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    s3 = np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x)
    s4 = np.sin(8.0 * np.pi * x)

    betas_fine = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 121)

    for s in (s1, s2, s3, s4):
        v = zero_mean_mode(base, S, t, s)
        for beta in betas_fine:
            a, sc, beta_used, vd = candidate_from_raw_v(v, base, S, beta, nfft)
            best_a, best_score, best_dir = update_best(top, best_a, best_score, a, sc, vd, beta_used)

    v1 = zero_mean_mode(base, S, t, s1)
    v2 = zero_mean_mode(base, S, t, s2)
    v3 = zero_mean_mode(base, S, t, s3)

    betas_med = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 61)
    betas_small = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 41)

    for c in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
        v = v1 + c * v2
        for beta in betas_med:
            a, sc, beta_used, vd = candidate_from_raw_v(v, base, S, beta, nfft)
            best_a, best_score, best_dir = update_best(top, best_a, best_score, a, sc, vd, beta_used)

        v = v1 + c * v3
        for beta in betas_small:
            a, sc, beta_used, vd = candidate_from_raw_v(v, base, S, beta, nfft)
            best_a, best_score, best_dir = update_best(top, best_a, best_score, a, sc, vd, beta_used)

    print("deterministic_grid best:", best_score, flush=True)
    return top, best_a, best_score, best_dir


def run_param_search(basis, base, S, nfft, best_a, best_score, best_dir, stop_time, rng, top):
    K = basis.shape[1]

    c = None
    if best_dir is not None and best_a is not None and np.isfinite(best_score):
        try:
            sol = np.linalg.lstsq(basis, best_dir, rcond=None)
            c = np.asarray(sol[0], dtype=np.float64).copy()
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
                c = None
        except Exception:
            c = None

    if c is None:
        c = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, K).astype(np.float64)

    beta = 0.5
    sigma = 0.25
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.05:
            c_new = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, K).astype(np.float64)
            beta_new = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 1.0))
        else:
            c_new = c + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, K)
            beta_new = beta + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08))
            if beta_new <= 0.0 or beta_new >= 1.0:
                if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
                    beta_new = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
                else:
                    beta_new = float(np.clip(beta_new, 0.0, 1.0))

        raw = basis @ c_new
        a, sc, beta_used, v = candidate_from_raw_v(raw, base, S, beta_new, nfft)

        if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            best_score = float(sc)
            c = c_new.copy()
            beta = float(beta_used)
            no_improve = 0
            sigma = min(0.6, sigma * 1.02)
            consider_top(top, a, sc, 5)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 5000:
                sigma = min(0.8, sigma * 1.1)
                no_improve = 0
            if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score + 0.01 and len(top) < 5:
                consider_top(top, a, sc, 5)

        it += 1
        if it % 20000 == 0:
            print("param_search it:", it, "best:", best_score, flush=True)

    return best_a, best_score


def run_local_search(n, nfft, best_a, best_score, stop_time, rng, top):
    a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    sc = float(best_score)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
        if it % 1000 == 0:
            sigma = float(0.02 + 0.08 * float(rng.random()))

        if float(rng.random()) < 0.65:
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            cand = a * np.exp(noise)
        else:
            cand = a.copy()
            width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 64), max(2, n // 8)))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            if stop > start:
                cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma * 1.5, stop - start))

        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(cand))
        if s > 1e-14:
            cand /= s
            score = objective_internal(cand, nfft)
            if np.isfinite(score) and score < sc:
                a = cand.copy()
                sc = float(score)
                best_a = cand.copy()
                best_score = score
                consider_top(top, cand, score, 5)

        it += 1
        if it % 20000 == 0:
            print("local_search it:", it, "best:", best_score, flush=True)

    return best_a, best_score


def exact_evaluate_top(top, default_a):
    best_a = None
    best_sc = float('inf')

    for item in top:
        _, a = item
        try:
            exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
        except Exception:
            exact = float('inf')

        if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(exact)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    if best_a is None and default_a is not None:
        try:
            exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in default_a])
        except Exception:
            exact = float('inf')

        if np.isfinite(exact):
            best_sc = float(exact)
            best_a = np.asarray(default_a, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    if best_a is None and default_a is not None:
        best_a = np.asarray(default_a, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    return best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Search over binomial-base modulations. The base sequence is the central
    binomial profile normalized to sum 1. Low-dimensional zero-mean antisymmetric
    weight functions are optimized by FFT-based evaluation, deterministic grids,
    random parametric search, and final local polishing.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    if budget_s is None:
        budget = 1800.0
    else:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    budget = max(30.0, budget)
    deadline = start + max(30.0, budget - 20.0)

    # Length 2535 matches the supplied construction scale and keeps FFT length
    # at 8192 while keeping vector operations moderate.
    n = 2535
    need = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < need:
        nfft <<= 1

    base = binomial_base(n)
    S = float(np.sum(base))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    top, best_a, best_score, best_dir = run_deterministic_grid(base, S, n, nfft)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    basis = build_basis(n)

    param_stop = deadline - 120.0
    if param_stop < start + 5.0:
        param_stop = deadline - 5.0

    if time.monotonic() < param_stop and best_a is not None:
        best_a, best_score = run_param_search(
            basis, base, S, nfft,
            best_a, best_score, best_dir,
            param_stop, rng, top
        )
        print("after_param_search best:", best_score, flush=True)

    local_stop = deadline - 5.0
    if time.monotonic() < local_stop and best_a is not None:
        best_a, best_score = run_local_search(
            n, nfft,
            best_a, best_score,
            local_stop, rng, top
        )
        print("after_local_search best:", best_score, flush=True)

    best_a = exact_evaluate_top(top, best_a)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = base / S

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_a = np.maximum(best_a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(best_a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    best_a /= s
    # The evaluator caps values at 1000. This sequence is normalized, so values
    # are far below that, but clip just in case of numerical anomalies.
    best_a = np.minimum(best_a, 1000.0)

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
